IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write. us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, if you think your statement is wrong or if you need more information about a transfer on the statement or receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error or problem appeared.
(1) Tell us your name and account number (it any).
(2) Describe the error or the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearly as you can why you bellave there is an error or why you need more information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error,
We will investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we take more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our investigationi.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your righte; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both upon request and In response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts. The following Information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is an error on your statement, write to us at; Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonia, WI 53036 or you may also email us.at csr@|xoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
(1) Your name and account number.
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected arror.
(3) If you think there is an error on your account, describe what you belleve is wrong and why you believe it is a mistake.
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may call us, but If you do.we are not required to investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we, investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question. or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount. But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other lees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance. 4} we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the periodic rate to the "dally balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees; and subtract any unpald Interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments. Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each.billing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.
Send payments and Inquires to address on front of bill.
Member
NOTE: Payments received after close of business shall be deemed received on the following business day for purposes of crediting your account. FDIC

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you'can, if you think your statement is wrong or if you need more Information about a transfer on the statement or receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error or problem appeared.
(1) Tell us your name and account number (il any).
(2) Describe the error of the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearly as you can why you believe there is an error or why you need more information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error.
We will investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we take more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our Investigation.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal.Fair Credit Billing Act will be cent le you both upon request and In response to a bliing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The, following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is an error on your statement, write to us al: Ixonla Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonia, WI 53036 or you may also email us at csr@ixoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
[1] Your name and account number.
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) if you think there is an error on your account, describe what you believe le wrong and why you believe it lo a mistake,
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may cas us, but If you do we are not required to investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been.an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may.remaln'on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount: But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other fees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have fo pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance. 4j we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the interest charge on your account by applying the periodic rate to the "daily balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "daily balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpaid interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives tis the daily balance,
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments, Payments shall be applied, first to any tinpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account: Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each billing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, if you think your statement is wrong or if-you need more information about a transfer on the statement or receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error or" problem. appeared.
(1) Tell us your name and account number (if any).
(2) Describe the error or the transfer you are tinsure about, and explain as clearly as you can why you believe there is an error or why you need more Information,
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error.
We will Investigate your complaint and will correct.any error promptly. If we lake more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our investigation.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both upon request and In response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account Is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The following Information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there Is an error on your statement, write to us at: Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, txonia, W| 53036 or you may also email us al csr@lxonlabank.com. In your letter, give us the following Information;
(1) Your name and account number.
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) if you think there is an error on your account, describe what you believe is wrong and why you believe.It lo a mistake,
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may cait us, but If you do we are not required to investigate any potential errors and,you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate wheiner or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount, 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interesl on that amount, But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other lees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance. 4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the periodic rate to the "daily balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpaid interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account Ican balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments. Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each billing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.
Send payments and Inquires to address on front of bill.

YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is is summary of your righte; a full'statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you, both upon request and In response to a bliling error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account, Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand. Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If. you think there is an error on your statement, write to us at; Ixonia Bank, PO Box :110, Ixonia, Wi 53036 or you may also email us al.csr@Ixoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
(1) Your name and account number,
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) If you think there Is an error on your account, describe what you bellove is wrong and why you believe it is a mistake.
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or'electronically). 'You may call us, but If you do we are not required to Investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. Walle we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as deliriquant on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount. Bul il we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or â€žany interest or other lees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance, 4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the pariodlo rate to the "dally balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract. any unpald Interest of other Binance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments. Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each bitting cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.

YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both'upon request and In response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated In conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are mada for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is an error on your statement, write to us at; (xonia Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonla, W[ 53036 or you may also emall us at osr@ |xoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following Information:
(1) Your name and account number.
(e) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) If you think there le an error on your account, describe what you believe te wrong and why you believe it is a mtatako.
You must contact us within 80 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may call us, but If you do we are not required to investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we, investigate whether or not there has been an error. the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain'on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interes! on that amount. But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other lees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance, 4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit,
We figure the interest charge on your account by applying the periodlo rate to the 'dally balance" of your, account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "daily balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpaid interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments. Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principel loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both upon request and in response to a billing error notica.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as ara made for Demand Deposit Accounts not cperated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts. The following Information thus apples only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there Is an error' on your statement, write to us at: Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, txonia, WI 53036.or you may also email us at csr@Ixonlabank.com. In your latter, give us the following Information:
(1) Your name and account number,
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) If you think there is an error on your account, describe what you believe la wrong and why you believe It Is a mistake.
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing for electronically). You may cas us, but If you do we are not required to Investigate any potential errors'and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest en that amount. But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other fees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance. 4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the pertodlo rate to the "daily balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees,, and subtract any unpaid Interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary 'additional payments, Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each bliling cycla showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.
Send payments and inquires to address on front of bill.

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, il you think your statement is wrong or if you need more Information about a transfer or the statement or receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error or problem appeared.
(1) Tell us your name and account number (if any).
(2) Describe the error or the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearly as you can why you bellava there is an error or why you need more Information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error.
We will Investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we-taka more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount. you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our investigation.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Crudit Billing Act will be sent lo you both upon request and In response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve-Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposil Account and they will be the same charges as are made lor Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts. The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is an error on your statement, write to us at: Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonia, WI 63036. or you may also email us.at csr@Ixoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
(1) Your name and account number.
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error, .
(3) If you think there is an error on your account, describe what you believe is wrong and why you believe It is a mistake,
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may call us, but "if you do we are not required to Investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true; 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interes! on that amount. Bul if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other lees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your belance, 4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the interest charge on your account by applying the perlodle rate to the "dally balance" of your account for each cay in the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpald interest or other finance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments. Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES; and second the principal loan balance outstanding In your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each builing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions,
Send payments and Inquires to address on front of bill.
Member
NOTE: Payments received after close of business shall be deemed received on the following business day for purposes of crediting your account. FDIC LENDER

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, if you think your statement is wrong or if you need more Information about a transter on the statement or receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error or problem appeared.
(1) Tell us your name and account number (if any).
(2) Describe the error or the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearly as you can why you believe there is an error or why you need more information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error.
We will investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we lake more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our Investigation.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both upon request and in response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts. The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is an error on your statement, write to us at: Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonia, WI 53036 or you may also email us at csr@ixoniabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
(1) Your name and account number.
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) If you think there Is an error on your account, describe what you believe is wrong and why you believe it is a mistake.
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors'in writing (or electronically). You may call us, but If you do we are not required to Investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount. But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay. the amount in question of any interest or other fees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance.'4) we can apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the interest charge on your account by applying the periodle rate to the "dally balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpaid interest or other finance charges and any payments or'credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make voluntary additional payments, Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each billing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account loan transactions.
Send payments and Inquires to address on front of bill.
Member
NOTE: Payments received after close of business shall be deemed received on the following business day for purposes of crediting your account. FDIC LENDCA

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, il you think your statement is wrong or if you need more information about a transter on the statement or'receipt. (1) Tell us your name and account number (if any).
(2) Describe the error of the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearty as you can why you believe there is an error or why you need more Information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error,
We will Investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we take more than 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time It takes us to complete out Investigation.
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your righte; a full statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent lo you both upon request and in response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand Deposit Account, Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Damand Deposit Account and they will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not operated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserve Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
I' you think there is an error on your statement, write to us al; Ixonla Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonla, WI 53036 or you may also emall us at csr@ixenlabank.com. In your letter, give us the following information:
(1) Your name and account number,
(2) The dollar amount of thÃ© suspected error.
(3) If you think there le an error on your account, describe what you believe is wrong and why you belove it is a mistake.
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statement. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing (or electronically). You may call us, but If you do we are not required to Investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount, But if we determine that we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other fees related to that amount. 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance. 4) we can. apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit,
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the periodic rate to the "dally balance" of your account for each day In the billing cycle. To get the "daily balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any unpaid Interest or other [inance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at any time, or make-voluntary additional payments, Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance. outstanding in your Personal Reserva Account. Periodic statements may be sent to you at the end of each billing cycle showing your Personal Reserve Account toan transactions.
Send payments and inquires to address on front of bill.

IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR ELECTRONIC TRANSFERS
Telephone or write us at the telephone number or address located on the front of this statement as soon as you can, if you think your statement is wrong or if you need more information about a transfer on the statement of receipt. We must hear from you no later than 60 days after we sent you the FIRST statement on which the error of problem appeared.
(1) Tel us your name and account number (if any).
(2) Dascribe the error or the transfer you are unsure about, and explain as clearty as you can why you believe there is an error or why you need more information.
(3) Tell us the dollar amount of the suspected error.
We will Investigate your complaint and will correct any error promptly. If we take more than' 10 business days to do this, we will recredit your account for the"amount you think is in error, so that you will have use of the money during the time it takes us to complete our Investigation .-
YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT SUMMARY OF RIGHTS IS OUTLINED BELOW
This is a summary of your rights; a full'statement of your rights and our responsibilities under the Federal Fair Credit Billing Act will be sent to you both upon; request and in response to a billing error notice.
Your Personal Reserve Account is operated in conjunction with your Demand. Deposit Account. Any charges for your checking account will be made to the Demand Deposit Account and thay will be the same charges as are made for Demand Deposit Accounts not. cperated in conjunction with Personal Reserve Accounts, The following information thus applies only to loans made to you under your Personal Reserva Account line of credit.
IN CASE OF ERRORS OR QUESTIONS ABOUT YOUR PERSONAL RESERVE ACCOUNT
If you think there is, an error on your statement, write-to-us at: Ixonia Bank, PO Box 110, Ixonia, WI 53036 or you may also email us al bsc@Ixonlabank.com; In your letter, give us the following Information:
(1) Your name and account number,
(2) The dollar amount of the suspected error.
(3) If you think there is an error on your account, describe what you bollove is wrong end why you beÅ¡ova It is a mistake,
You must contact us within 60 days after the error appeared on your statemant. You must notify us of any potential errors in writing [or etactronicatty). You may call us, but if you do we' are not required to investigate any potential errors and you may have to pay the amount in question. While we investigate whether or not there has been an error, the following are true: 1) we cannot try to collect the amount in question, or report you as delinquent on that amount. 2) the charge in question may remain on your statement, and we may continue to charge you interest on that amount. Bul if we determine that'we made a mistake, you will not have to pay the amount in question or any interest or other lees related to that amount: 3) while you do not have to pay the amount in question, you are responsible for the remainder of your balance : 4] we can, apply any unpaid amount against your credit limit.
We figure the Interest charge on your account by applying the periodic rate to the "daily balance" ct your account for each day in the billing cycle. To get the. "dally balance" we take the beginning balance of your account each day, add any new purchases, advances, or fees, and subtract any uripald Interest or other linance charges and any payments or credits. This gives us the daily balance.
The minimum periodic payment required is shown on the front-of this bill. You may pay off your Personal Reserve Account loan balances at dily time, or make.voluntary additional payments, Payments shall be applied, first to any unpaid FINANCE CHARGES, and second the principal loan balance outstanding in your Personal Reserve Account.
